HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_217508058b7d324d1135","text":"1. **Authority joined to tested inner knowledge.** `ر ب ب B001` supplies lordship, ownership, and authority, while `خ ب ر B001` supplies tested knowledge of an affair's inward reality rather than outward appearance. The emphatic clause and repeated human pronouns place that knowledge directly upon the people after graves and breasts have been opened. Authority is the relation; inward expertise is the mode of oversight. The packet supplies both roles; I infer that competent authority encompasses the disclosed inner account. `لَّخَبِيرٌ` reads as penetrating, tested knowledge appropriate to lordship, not knowledge by distant report.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B011","root_000822/B002","root_001321/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2c47efd8e3a372aa91dc","text":"3. **Covenant breached and expertly known.** Evidence: ر ب ب B011 covenant/pact, B016 need/favor, B001 authority; ك ن د B001 severing and B002 ingratitude; ش ه د B002 testimony. Structural cue: 100:6 states ingratitude \"to his Rabb\"; 100:11 closes with their Rabb knowing them. Role: Rabb as covenant-holder frames kanud as breach of protected relation. Abductive move: favor creates an implicit pact; cutting gratitude violates it and calls for expert testimony. Reading change: the final ayah reads as covenantal disclosure: the Lord knows the breach from inside the relation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4e27ceaeec260c3c1724","text":"No later ayah exists in this fixed ayah's clipped focus window, so no later-ayah surprise emerged.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B001","root_000532/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5a4816c6d49f1152ff5e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:11:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]],[\"100:11:5\",\"root_000387\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Aşama aşama yetiştiren Rab, insanların gelişimini ve nimete verdikleri karşılığı yalnız o gün öğrenmez; ilişki boyunca içeriden bilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_78020d1e7de349035882","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:11:5\",\"root_000387\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Rabbin bilgisi, dağılan delillerin toplanıp yerleştiği yumuşak bir zemin gibi tasarlanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B002","root_000532/B008","root_000532/B013","root_001544/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_786b8063e37eab1fe557","text":"2. **Soft ground receiving the revealed contents.** Evidence: خ ب ر B002 soft low water-gathering ground; ر ب ب B013 abundant collected water and B008 low cloud; ن ق ع B001 settled water, B007 level clay flats. Structural cue: the surah's hard/dusty motion is countered by low wet gathering imagery in the final name. Role: khabir as soft ground receives what has been churned up; rabb branches supply cloud/water nurture. Abductive move: divine knowledge is like a receptive low place where dispersed evidence settles and becomes knowable. Reading change: \"khabir\" gains an environmental mechanism of gathering and absorbing traces.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B001","root_000532/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_827f02a37812610f74ad","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:11:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\"]],[\"100:11:5\",\"root_000387\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rabbin sahipliği ve otoritesi, insanların dış görünüşünü değil sınanmış iç gerçekliğini bilen uzmanlıkla birleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B001","root_000532/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a9551300fff66ffbc610","text":"2. **Nurture makes the knowledge longitudinal and relational.** `ر ب ب B002` supplies stage-by-stage nurture, repair, and completion. Joined to `خ ب ر B001`, it creates knowledge acquired through sustained care of the same subjects, while 100:6-8 show their ingratitude and redirected attachment. The nurturer's continued relation supplies the basis for knowing development and response over time. The packet does not state that care causes acquaintance; that is my abductive arrow. The final predicate can therefore read as intimate knowledge of a nurtured history, not a new discovery made only `يَوْمَئِذٍ`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_aba122e1843d3adb606b","text":"As the final ayah, it received rather than sent later activation. Its retrospective surprise is internal: prior dust, water, grave, chest, witness, and covenant branches all converge in خبير, making the divine knowledge feel like the surah's collecting basin and final expert verdict.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B011","root_000532/B016"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b712ec00df730cb2fc13","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:11:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B011\",\"B016\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Rab ile insan arasındaki nimet ilişkisi bir sözleşme gibi görünür; nankörlük bu bağı ihlal eder ve ihlal ilişkinin içinden bilinir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B003","root_001321/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d031afd4468309898ad0","text":"3. **Exploratory cultivation and harvest reading.** `خ ب ر B003` includes the expert cultivator or sharecropper, and `ر ب ب B002` supplies nurture. In the same fixed window, `ك ن د B003` gives barren land, `ح ب ب B001` seed, `خ ي ر B005` beneficence, and `ح ص ل B001`, `B002`, and `B003` the resolved yield, extracted value, or residue. These branches form cultivator, ground, seed or gift, and harvest-account. The packet supplies the complete ecological inventory; I infer that it models the human response to nurture. On this coexisting reading, `رَبَّهُم ... لَّخَبِيرٌ` is the expert cultivator who already knows the ground and accurately assesses what its inward crop has yielded.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B001","root_000532/B001","root_000532/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e686f458ec8971496a79","text":"1. **Nurturing owner as tested knower.** Evidence: ر ب ب B001 lordship/ownership and B002 nurture/completion; خ ب ر B001 report, inquiry, tested inner knowledge. Structural cue: rabb appears in 100:6 as the one toward whom the human is kanud, and returns with plural pronouns after exposure. Role: Rabb is both caretaker-owner and expert examiner; khabir names inward verified knowledge. Abductive move: the one who nurtured the process stage by stage is uniquely competent to know its results. Reading change: the final ayah reads as care-based expertise, not detached surveillance alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B003","root_000532/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_edc269559041fb37000d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:11:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]],[\"100:11:5\",\"root_000387\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Toprak, tohum ve ürün çağrışımları içinde Rab, yetiştirdiği zeminin ne verdiğini doğru değerlendiren uzman bir ekici gibi de belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
